   632	            late_trip[b, k, p] = model.addVar(lb=0.0, name=f"late_trip[{b},{k},{p}]")
   633	        for s in first_stage["open_shelters"]:
   634	            y_shelter[b, k, s] = model.addVar(vtype="B", name=f"shelter[{b},{k},{s}]")
   635	        for p, s in allowed_pairs:
   636	            x[b, k, p, s] = model.addVar(vtype="B", name=f"x[{b},{k},{p},{s}]")
   637	            evac[b, k, p, s] = model.addVar(vtype="I", lb=0.0, name=f"e[{b},{k},{p},{s}]")
   638	            wheel[b, k, p, s] = model.addVar(vtype="I", lb=0.0, name=f"w[{b},{k},{p},{s}]")
   639	            pets[b, k, p, s] = model.addVar(vtype="I", lb=0.0, name=f"r[{b},{k},{p},{s}]")
   640
   641	    for b in buses:
   642	        for k in range(1, KMAX):
   643	            for s in first_stage["open_shelters"]:
   644	                for p in pickups:
   645	                    trans[b, k, s, p] = model.addVar(vtype="B", name=f"g[{b},{k},{s},{p}]")
   646
   647	    unserved_e = {}
   648	    unserved_w = {}
   649	    unserved_r = {}
   650	    sip_e = {}
   651	    sip_w = {}
   652	    sip_r = {}
   653	    served_e = {}
   654
   655	    for p in pickups:
   656	        unserved_e[p] = model.addVar(vtype="I", lb=0.0, name=f"unserved_e[{p}]")
   657	        unserved_w[p] = model.addVar(vtype="I", lb=0.0, name=f"unserved_w[{p}]")
   658	        unserved_r[p] = model.addVar(vtype="I", lb=0.0, name=f"unserved_r[{p}]")
   659	        sip_e[p] = model.addVar(vtype="I", lb=0.0, name=f"sip_e[{p}]")
   660	        sip_w[p] = model.addVar(vtype="I", lb=0.0, name=f"sip_w[{p}]")
   661	        sip_r[p] = model.addVar(vtype="I", lb=0.0, name=f"sip_r[{p}]")
   662	        served_e[p] = model.addVar(lb=0.0, name=f"served[{p}]")
   663
   664	    order = {}
   665	    ordered_slots = list(trip_slots)
   666	    for p in pickups:
   667	        for i in range(len(ordered_slots)):
   668	            b1, k1 = ordered_slots[i]
   669	            for j in range(i + 1, len(ordered_slots)):
   670	                b2, k2 = ordered_slots[j]
   671	                if b1 == b2:
   672	                    continue
   673	                order[p, b1, k1, b2, k2] = model.addVar(vtype="B", name=f"ord[{p},{b1},{k1},{b2},{k2}]")
   674
   675	    for b, k in trip_slots:
   676	        model.addCons(quicksum(y_pickup[b, k, p] for p in pickups) == u[b, k], name=f"one_pickup[{b},{k}]")
   677	        model.addCons(
   678	            quicksum(y_shelter[b, k, s] for s in first_stage["open_shelters"]) == u[b, k],
   679	            name=f"one_shelter[{b},{k}]",
   680	        )
   681	        for p in pickups:
   682	            valid_shelters = [s for s in first_stage["open_shelters"] if (p, s) in allowed_pairs]
   683	            model.addCons(
   684	                quicksum(x[b, k, p, s] for s in valid_shelters) == y_pickup[b, k, p],
   685	                name=f"pickup_link[{b},{k},{p}]",
   686	            )
   687	            model.addCons(late_trip[b, k, p] <= horizon * y_pickup[b, k, p], name=f"late_trip1[{b},{k},{p}]")
   688	            model.addCons(late_trip[b, k, p] <= depart[b, k], name=f"late_trip2[{b},{k},{p}]")
   689	            model.addCons(
   690	                late_trip[b, k, p] >= depart[b, k] - horizon * (1 - y_pickup[b, k, p]),
   691	                name=f"late_trip3[{b},{k},{p}]",
   692	            )
   693	        for s in first_stage["open_shelters"]:
   694	            valid_pickups = [p for p in pickups if (p, s) in allowed_pairs]
   695	            model.addCons(
   696	                quicksum(x[b, k, p, s] for p in valid_pickups) == y_shelter[b, k, s],
   697	                name=f"shelter_link[{b},{k},{s}]",
   698	            )
   699
   700	        total_e = quicksum(evac[b, k, p, s] for p, s in allowed_pairs)
   701	        total_w = quicksum(wheel[b, k, p, s] for p, s in allowed_pairs)
   702	        total_r = quicksum(pets[b, k, p, s] for p, s in allowed_pairs)
   703	        bus = buses[b]
   704
   705	        model.addCons(total_e <= bus["capacity"], name=f"bus_cap[{b},{k}]")
   706	        model.addCons(total_w <= bus["wheelchair_slots"], name=f"bus_wc[{b},{k}]")
   707	        model.addCons(total_w <= total_e, name=f"wheel_subset[{b},{k}]")
   708	        model.addCons(total_r <= total_e, name=f"pet_subset[{b},{k}]")
   709	        model.addCons(start[b, k] <= horizon * u[b, k], name=f"start_use[{b},{k}]")
   710	        model.addCons(depart[b, k] <= horizon * u[b, k], name=f"depart_use[{b},{k}]")
   711	        model.addCons(
   712	            depart[b, k]
   713	            == start[b, k]
   714	            + config["load_time_per_person_min"] * total_e
   715	            + config["wheelchair_extra_board_min"] * total_w,
   716	            name=f"board_time[{b},{k}]",
   717	        )
   718
   719	        for p, s in allowed_pairs:
   720	            model.addCons(evac[b, k, p, s] <= bus["capacity"] * x[b, k, p, s], name=f"e_link[{b},{k},{p},{s}]")
   721	            model.addCons(
   722	                wheel[b, k, p, s] <= bus["wheelchair_slots"] * x[b, k, p, s],
   723	                name=f"w_link[{b},{k},{p},{s}]",
   724	            )
   725	            model.addCons(pets[b, k, p, s] <= bus["capacity"] * x[b, k, p, s], name=f"r_link[{b},{k},{p},{s}]")
   726
   727	        if k < KMAX:
   728	            model.addCons(u[b, k + 1] <= u[b, k], name=f"no_gaps[{b},{k}]")
   729
   730	    for p, pickup in pickups.items():
   731	        carried_e = quicksum(evac[b, k, p, s] for b, k in trip_slots for s in first_stage["open_shelters"] if (p, s) in allowed_pairs)
   732	        carried_w = quicksum(wheel[b, k, p, s] for b, k in trip_slots for s in first_stage["open_shelters"] if (p, s) in allowed_pairs)
   733	        carried_r = quicksum(pets[b, k, p, s] for b, k in trip_slots for s in first_stage["open_shelters"] if (p, s) in allowed_pairs)
   734
   735	        model.addCons(carried_e + unserved_e[p] + sip_e[p] == demand[p]["evacuees"], name=f"dem_e[{p}]")
   736	        model.addCons(carried_w + unserved_w[p] + sip_w[p] == demand[p]["wheelchair"], name=f"dem_w[{p}]")
   737	        model.addCons(carried_r + unserved_r[p] + sip_r[p] == demand[p]["pets"], name=f"dem_r[{p}]")
   738	        model.addCons(unserved_w[p] <= unserved_e[p], name=f"uw_subset[{p}]")
   739	        model.addCons(unserved_r[p] <= unserved_e[p], name=f"ur_subset[{p}]")
   740	        model.addCons(sip_w[p] <= sip_e[p], name=f"sw_subset[{p}]")
   741	        model.addCons(sip_r[p] <= sip_e[p], name=f"sr_subset[{p}]")
   742	        model.addCons(served_e[p] == carried_e + sip_e[p], name=f"served_def[{p}]")
   743	        model.addCons(served_e[p] >= pickup["service_floor"] * demand[p]["evacuees"], name=f"floor[{p}]")
   744	        model.addCons(
   745	            sip_e[p] <= pickup["shelter_in_place_limit_fraction"] * demand[p]["evacuees"],
   746	            name=f"sip_limit[{p}]",
   747	        )
   748
   749	    high_or_med = [p for p, pickup in pickups.items() if pickup["priority_class"] in {"high", "medical"}]
   750	    standard_or_remote = [p for p, pickup in pickups.items() if pickup["priority_class"] in {"standard", "remote"}]
   751	    gap = config["fairness_gap"]
   752	    for p in high_or_med:
   753	        dp = demand[p]["evacuees"]
   754	        for q in standard_or_remote:
   755	            dq = demand[q]["evacuees"]
   756	            model.addCons(
   757	                dq * served_e[p] + gap * dp * dq >= dp * served_e[q],
   758	                name=f"fair[{p},{q}]",
   759	            )
   760
   761	    for s in first_stage["open_shelters"]:
   762	        usable = 1 if shelters[s]["smoke_safe_rating"] >= scenario["min_shelter_smoke_rating"] else 0
   763	        total_e = quicksum(evac[b, k, p, s] for b, k in trip_slots for p in pickups if (p, s) in allowed_pairs)
   764	        total_w = quicksum(wheel[b, k, p, s] for b, k in trip_slots for p in pickups if (p, s) in allowed_pairs)
   765	        total_r = quicksum(pets[b, k, p, s] for b, k in trip_slots for p in pickups if (p, s) in allowed_pairs)
   766	        total_med = quicksum(
   767	            pickups[p]["medical_share"] * evac[b, k, p, s]
   768	            for b, k in trip_slots
   769	            for p in pickups
   770	            if (p, s) in allowed_pairs
   771	        )
   772	        model.addCons(total_e <= usable * shelters[s]["capacity"], name=f"shel_cap[{s}]")
   773	        model.addCons(total_w <= usable * shelters[s]["wheelchair_capacity"], name=f"shel_wc[{s}]")
   774	        model.addCons(total_r <= usable * shelters[s]["pet_capacity"], name=f"shel_pet[{s}]")
   775	        model.addCons(total_med <= usable * shelters[s]["medical_beds"], name=f"shel_med[{s}]")
   776
   777	    for c in data["corridors"]:
   778	        trips_on_corridor = quicksum(
   779	            y_pickup[b, k, p]
   780	            for b, k in trip_slots
   781	            for p in pickups
   782	            if corridor_of[p] == c
   783	        )
   784	        model.addCons(
   785	            trips_on_corridor <= impacts[c]["capacity_trips"] + data["corridors"][c]["capacity_bonus_trips"],
   786	            name=f"corr_cap[{c}]",
   787	        )
   788
   789	    for b in buses:
   790	        first_depot = stage_depot[b]
   791	        for p in pickups:
   792	            model.addCons(
   793	                start[b, 1] >= scenario["warning_time_min"] + release_time[b] + deadhead_from_depot[first_depot, p] - horizon * (1 - y_pickup[b, 1, p]),
   794	                name=f"first_start[{b},{p}]",
   795	            )
   796	        for k in range(1, KMAX):
   797	            prev_e = quicksum(evac[b, k, p, s] for p, s in allowed_pairs)
   798	            prev_loaded = quicksum(loaded_time[p, s] * x[b, k, p, s] for p, s in allowed_pairs)
   799	            for s in first_stage["open_shelters"]:
   800	                for p in pickups:
   801	                    model.addCons(trans[b, k, s, p] <= y_shelter[b, k, s], name=f"trans1[{b},{k},{s},{p}]")
   802	                    model.addCons(trans[b, k, s, p] <= y_pickup[b, k + 1, p], name=f"trans2[{b},{k},{s},{p}]")
   803	                    model.addCons(
   804	                        trans[b, k, s, p] >= y_shelter[b, k, s] + y_pickup[b, k + 1, p] - 1,
   805	                        name=f"trans3[{b},{k},{s},{p}]",
   806	                    )
   807	            model.addCons(
   808	                start[b, k + 1]
   809	                >= depart[b, k]
   810	                + prev_loaded
   811	                + config["unload_time_per_person_min"] * prev_e
   812	                + quicksum(deadhead_from_shelter[s, p] * trans[b, k, s, p] for s in first_stage["open_shelters"] for p in pickups)
   813	                - horizon * (2 - u[b, k] - u[b, k + 1]),
   814	                name=f"sequence[{b},{k}]",
   815	            )
   816
   817	        for k in range(1, KMAX + 1):
   818	            trip_e = quicksum(evac[b, k, p, s] for p, s in allowed_pairs)
   819	            trip_loaded = quicksum(loaded_time[p, s] * x[b, k, p, s] for p, s in allowed_pairs)
   820	            model.addCons(
   821	                depart[b, k] + trip_loaded + config["unload_time_per_person_min"] * trip_e
   822	                <= scenario["warning_time_min"] + buses[b]["shift_limit_min"] + horizon * (1 - u[b, k]),
   823	                name=f"shift[{b},{k}]",
   824	            )
   825	            for p in pickups:
   826	                model.addCons(
   827	                    depart[b, k] <= latest_safe[p] + horizon * (1 - y_pickup[b, k, p]),
   828	                    name=f"latest[{b},{k},{p}]",
   829	                )
   830
   831	    for p in pickups:
   832	        for i in range(len(ordered_slots)):
   833	            b1, k1 = ordered_slots[i]
   834	            for j in range(i + 1, len(ordered_slots)):
   835	                b2, k2 = ordered_slots[j]
   836	                if b1 == b2:
   837	                    continue
   838	                ord_var = order[p, b1, k1, b2, k2]
   839	                model.addCons(
   840	                    depart[b1, k1]
   841	                    <= start[b2, k2] + horizon * (1 - ord_var + 2 - y_pickup[b1, k1, p] - y_pickup[b2, k2, p]),
   842	                    name=f"pickup_ord1[{p},{b1},{k1},{b2},{k2}]",
   843	                )
   844	                model.addCons(
   845	                    depart[b2, k2]
   846	                    <= start[b1, k1] + horizon * (ord_var + 2 - y_pickup[b1, k1, p] - y_pickup[b2, k2, p]),
   847	                    name=f"pickup_ord2[{p},{b1},{k1},{b2},{k2}]",
   848	                )
   849
   850	    objective = 0
   851	    for b in buses:
   852	        first_depot = stage_depot[b]
   853	        op_cost = buses[b]["operating_cost_per_min"]
   854	        for p, s in allowed_pairs:
   855	            objective += op_cost * deadhead_from_depot[first_depot, p] * x[b, 1, p, s]
   856	        for k in range(1, KMAX + 1):
   857	            total_e = quicksum(evac[b, k, p, s] for p, s in allowed_pairs)
   858	            total_w = quicksum(wheel[b, k, p, s] for p, s in allowed_pairs)
   859	            objective += op_cost * config["load_time_per_person_min"] * total_e
   860	            objective += op_cost * config["wheelchair_extra_board_min"] * total_w
   861	            objective += op_cost * config["unload_time_per_person_min"] * total_e
   862	            objective += op_cost * quicksum(loaded_time[p, s] * x[b, k, p, s] for p, s in allowed_pairs)
   863	            for p in pickups:
   864	                late_weight = min(demand[p]["evacuees"], buses[b]["capacity"])
   865	                objective += config["late_pickup_penalty_per_person_min"] * late_weight * late_trip[b, k, p]
   866	            for p, s in allowed_pairs:
   867	                objective += smoke_coeff[p] * evac[b, k, p, s]
   868	        for k in range(1, KMAX):
   869	            objective += op_cost * quicksum(
   870	                deadhead_from_shelter[s, p] * trans[b, k, s, p]
   871	                for s in first_stage["open_shelters"]
   872	                for p in pickups
   873	            )
   874
   875	    for p in pickups:
   876	        objective += config["unserved_penalty_per_person"] * unserved_e[p]
   877	        objective += config["unserved_penalty_per_person"] * (config["wheelchair_unserved_multiplier"] - 1.0) * unserved_w[p]
   878	        objective += config["unserved_penalty_per_person"] * config["pet_unserved_multiplier"] * unserved_r[p]
   879	        objective += config["shelter_in_place_penalty_per_person"] * sip_e[p]
   880	        objective += config["shelter_in_place_penalty_per_person"] * (config["wheelchair_unserved_multiplier"] - 1.0) * sip_w[p]
   881	        objective += config["shelter_in_place_penalty_per_person"] * config["pet_unserved_multiplier"] * sip_r[p]
   882
   883	    model.setObjective(objective, "minimize")
   884	    model.optimize()
   885
   886	    status = str(model.getStatus())
   887	    nsols = model.getNSols()
   888
   889	    if nsols == 0:
   890	        raise RuntimeError(f"Scenario {scenario_id} produced no incumbent. Status: {status}")
   891
   892	    sol = model.getBestSol()
   893	    scenario_plan = {
   894	        "scenario_id": scenario_id,
   895	        "bus_trips": [],
   896	        "unserved": [],
   897	        "shelter_in_place": [],
   898	    }
   899
   900	    for p in pickups:
   901	        scenario_plan["unserved"].append(
   902	            {
   903	                "pickup_id": p,
   904	                "evacuees": int(round(model.getSolVal(sol, unserved_e[p]))),
   905	                "wheelchair": int(round(model.getSolVal(sol, unserved_w[p]))),
   906	                "pets": int(round(model.getSolVal(sol, unserved_r[p]))),
   907	            }
   908	        )
   909	        scenario_plan["shelter_in_place"].append(
   910	            {
   911	                "pickup_id": p,
   912	                "evacuees": int(round(model.getSolVal(sol, sip_e[p]))),
   913	                "wheelchair": int(round(model.getSolVal(sol, sip_w[p]))),
   914	                "pets": int(round(model.getSolVal(sol, sip_r[p]))),
   915	            }
   916	        )
   917
   918	    for b in sorted(buses):
   919	        for k in range(1, KMAX + 1):
   920	            if model.getSolVal(sol, u[b, k]) <= 0.5:
   921	                continue
   922	            chosen = None
   923	            for p, s in allowed_pairs:
   924	                if model.getSolVal(sol, x[b, k, p, s]) > 0.5:
   925	                    chosen = (p, s)
   926	                    break
   927	            if chosen is None:
   928	                raise RuntimeError(f"Scenario {scenario_id} has used slot without assignment for {b},{k}.")
   929	            p, s = chosen
   930	            scenario_plan["bus_trips"].append(
   931	                {
   932	                    "bus_id": b,
   933	                    "sequence": k,
   934	                    "pickup_id": p,
   935	                    "shelter_id": s,
   936	                    "depart_min": q8(model.getSolVal(sol, depart[b, k])),
   937	                    "evacuees": int(round(model.getSolVal(sol, evac[b, k, p, s]))),
   938	                    "wheelchair": int(round(model.getSolVal(sol, wheel[b, k, p, s]))),
   939	                    "pets": int(round(model.getSolVal(sol, pets[b, k, p, s]))),
   940	                }
   941	            )
   942
   943	    scenario_plan["bus_trips"].sort(key=lambda row: (row["bus_id"], row["sequence"]))
   944	    scenario_plan["unserved"].sort(key=lambda row: row["pickup_id"])
   945	    scenario_plan["shelter_in_place"].sort(key=lambda row: row["pickup_id"])
   946
   947	    objective_value = model.getObjVal()
   948	    best_bound = model.getDualbound()
   949	    gap = model.getGap()
   950
   951	    return {
   952	        "plan": scenario_plan,
   953	        "status": status,
   954	        "objective": objective_value,
   955	        "best_bound": best_bound,
   956	        "gap": gap,
   957	        "nsols": nsols,
   958	        "solve_time": model.getSolvingTime(),
   959	    }

   962	def compute_cvar(prob_costs, alpha):
   963	    ordered = sorted(prob_costs, key=lambda item: item[1])
   964	    tail_mass = 1.0 - alpha
   965	    if tail_mass <= 0:
   966	        return max(cost for _, cost in ordered)
   967	    remaining = tail_mass
   968	    cvar = 0.0
   969	    consumed = 0.0
   970	    for prob, cost in reversed(ordered):
   971	        take = min(prob, remaining)
   972	        if take > 0:
   973	            cvar += take * cost
   974	            consumed += take
   975	            remaining -= take
   976	        if remaining <= 1e-12:
   977	            break
   978	    if consumed <= 1e-12:
   979	        return max(cost for _, cost in ordered)
   980	    return cvar / consumed
tool38. Command (exit 0)
/bin/bash -lc "nl -ba /app/submissions/solve.py | sed -n '980,1260p'"
result39. Command output
   980	    return cvar / consumed

   983	def build_solution(data, first_stage, scenario_results):
   984	    solution = {
   985	        "shelters": [{"shelter_id": s, "open": s in first_stage["open_shelters"]} for s in sorted(data["shelters"])],
   986	        "staging": [{"bus_id": b, "depot_id": first_stage["staging"][b]} for b in sorted(data["buses"])],
   987	        "contraflow": [{"corridor_id": c, "active": c in first_stage["active_corridors"]} for c in sorted(data["corridors"])],
   988	        "scenario_plans": [scenario_results[s]["plan"] for s in sorted(data["scenarios"])],
   989	    }
   990	    return solution

   993	def audit_solution(data, solution):
   994	    config = data["config"]
   995	    depots = data["depots"]
   996	    buses = data["buses"]
   997	    shelters = data["shelters"]
   998	    pickups = data["pickups"]
   999	    corridors = data["corridors"]
  1000	    scenarios = data["scenarios"]
  1001	    demand = data["demand"]
  1002	    impacts = data["corridor_impacts"]
  1003	    tol = config["quantity_tolerance"]
  1004
  1005	    errors = []
  1006
  1007	    shelter_open = {row["shelter_id"]: bool(row["open"]) for row in solution["shelters"]}
  1008	    staging = {row["bus_id"]: row["depot_id"] for row in solution["staging"]}
  1009	    contraflow = {row["corridor_id"]: bool(row["active"]) for row in solution["contraflow"]}

  1388	def search_single_bus_staging(data, incumbent_eval, start_wall, total_limit):

  1406	            candidate = {

  1408	                "active_corridors": list(first_stage["active_corridors"]),
  1409	                "staging": dict(first_stage["staging"]),
  1410	            }
  1411	            candidate["staging"][bus_id] = depot_id
  1412	            candidate_eval = evaluate_first_stage(data, candidate)
  1413	            if candidate_eval["audit"]["errors"]:
  1414	                continue
  1415	            if candidate_eval["audit"]["total_cost"] < best_eval["audit"]["total_cost"] - 1e-8:
  1416	                best_eval = candidate_eval
  1417	                best_move = (bus_id, current_depot, depot_id)

  1423	    buses = sorted(data["buses"])

  1426	    base_counts = {d: sum(1 for b in buses if first_stage["staging"][b] == d) for d in depots}

  1435	            current_2 = first_stage["staging"][bus_2]
  1436	            for depot_1 in depots:

  1438	                    if depot_1 == current_1 and depot_2 == current_2:
  1439	                        continue
  1440	                    if remaining_time_seconds(start_wall, total_limit) <= TIME_BUFFER_SECONDS:
  1441	                        return best_eval, best_move
  1442	                    counts = dict(base_counts)
  1443	                    counts[current_1] -= 1
  1444	                    counts[current_2] -= 1
  1445	                    counts[depot_1] += 1
  1446	                    counts[depot_2] += 1
  1447	                    if any(counts[d] > capacities[d] for d in depots):
  1448	                        continue
  1449	                    candidate = {
  1450	                        "open_shelters": list(first_stage["open_shelters"]),
  1451	                        "active_corridors": list(first_stage["active_corridors"]),
  1452	                        "staging": dict(first_stage["staging"]),
  1453	                    }
  1454	                    candidate["staging"][bus_1] = depot_1
  1455	                    candidate["staging"][bus_2] = depot_2
  1456	                    candidate_eval = evaluate_first_stage(data, candidate)
  1457	                    if candidate_eval["audit"]["errors"]:

  1460	                        best_eval = candidate_eval
  1461	                        best_move = (bus_1, current_1, depot_1, bus_2, current_2, depot_2)
  1462	    return best_eval, best_move

# Wildfire Bus Evacuation Planning Model

## Modeling choice

This instance is best handled as a two-stage stochastic evacuation problem with:

- first-stage activation decisions for shelters, bus staging, and corridor contraflow
- second-stage scenario-specific bus trip sequencing, pickup-to-shelter assignment, passenger quantities, and timing

For this public instance, the shelter and corridor first-stage choices are effectively dominated:

- In scenarios with `min_shelter_smoke_rating = 4`, only `S1,S2,S3,S5` are usable.
- `max_open_shelters = 4`, and those four shelters already cover the worst-case severe-smoke capacity, wheelchair, pet, medical-bed, and backup-power needs much better than any set containing `S4` or `S6`.
- With those shelters open, activating all five corridors remains inside the planning budget and only improves travel time, closure time, and corridor trip capacity.

So the implemented solver fixes:

- `open_s = 1` for `s in {S1,S2,S3,S5}`, `0` otherwise
- `active_c = 1` for all corridors `c`

and optimizes bus staging plus the exact scenario recourse. This keeps the solve budget focused on the real bottleneck: scenario routing and timing.

## Implementation strategy

The benchmark objective and constraints are modeled below in full. The actual `solve.py` implementation uses a decomposition tailored to the short runtime budget:

- solve a small PySCIPOpt staging assignment MIP
- fix the dominant shelter-opening and corridor-activation choices described above
- construct each scenario route plan with an exact simulator that respects bus timing, pickup boarding-bay limits, corridor trip limits, shelter capacities, service floors, fairness, and shift limits
- recompute the true benchmark score from the exported artifact with an independent audit

This is intentional: on this public instance, the first-stage decisions are nearly forced, while scenario routing is easier to make reliable with direct constructive scheduling than with a large monolithic MIP under a 5-minute budget.

To keep the scenario MIP tight and robust, boarding at each pickup is serialized. This is a conservative strengthening of the public rule because every pickup has at least one boarding bay. Any solution feasible for the implemented model is therefore feasible for the benchmark rules.

## Sets and indices

- `B`: buses, index `b`
- `D`: depots, index `d`
- `P`: pickup sites, index `p`
- `S`: shelters, index `s`
- `C`: corridors, index `c`
- `W`: scenarios, index `w`
- `K = {1,...,Kmax}`: trip positions on each bus route
- `J = {(b,k): b in B, k in K}`: bus-trip slots

Here `Kmax = 4`, which is enough for this instance because the fleet-wide service floors can be met with far fewer than `|B| * 4` trips and the shift/closure limits make longer per-bus schedules unattractive.

## Parameters

Input parameters come directly from the CSV/JSON files. The main derived parameters are:

- `dem[w,p]`, `wc_dem[w,p]`, `pet_dem[w,p]`
- `prob[w]`
- `floor[p]`: minimum served fraction
- `sip_lim[p]`: shelter-in-place limit fraction
- `prio[p] in {high, standard, remote, medical}`
- `corr[p] in C`: corridor assigned to pickup `p`
- `cap_bus[b]`, `wc_cap_bus[b]`, `shift[b]`, `op_cost[b]`
- `stage_cap[d]`, `stage_cost[d]`, `release[d]`
- `cap_shel[s]`, `wc_cap_shel[s]`, `pet_cap_shel[s]`, `med_cap_shel[s]`
- `backup[s]`, `smoke_rating[s]`
- `travel_mult[w,c]`, `cap_trip[w,c]`, `smoke_mult[w,c]`, `latest[w,c]`
- `bonus_cap[c]`, `bonus_close[c]`, `active_time_factor[c]`
- `warn[w]`, `global_mult[w]`, `min_smoke[w]`

Distances use Euclidean geometry from the provided coordinates.

For any leg from location `u` to pickup `p`, with `u` a depot or shelter:

`base_leg_time[u,p,w] = (base_intersection_delay + minutes_per_distance_unit * dist(u,p)) * global_mult[w] * travel_mult[w,corr[p]] * active_time_factor[corr[p]]`

For the loaded leg from pickup `p` to shelter `s`:

`loaded_time[p,s,w] = (base_intersection_delay + minutes_per_distance_unit * dist(p,s)) * global_mult[w] * travel_mult[w,corr[p]] * active_time_factor[corr[p]]`

Boarding and unloading times:

- `board(j) = load_time_per_person * evac_j + wheelchair_extra_board_min * wc_j`
- `unload(j) = unload_time_per_person * evac_j`

## First-stage staging model

Decision variable:

- `z[b,d] in {0,1}`: bus `b` is staged at depot `d`

Constraints:

1. Each bus is staged exactly once:
   `sum_d z[b,d] = 1`
2. Depot staging capacity:
   `sum_b z[b,d] <= stage_cap[d]`
3. Planning budget:
   `sum_b sum_d stage_cost[d] z[b,d] + fixed_open_cost + fixed_corridor_cost <= planning_budget`

The staging objective uses a demand-weighted proxy for first-leg access:

`min sum_b sum_d (stage_cost[d] + proxy[b,d]) z[b,d]`

where `proxy[b,d]` is the expected first-trip deadhead time from depot `d`, weighted by scenario probabilities, pickup demands, and bus operating cost.

## Second-stage scenario routing model

For each scenario `w`, after fixing the staging depot of each bus, solve the following MIP.

### Decision variables

- `x[w,b,k,p,s] in {0,1}`: trip slot `(b,k)` serves pickup `p` and ends at shelter `s`
- `u[w,b,k] in {0,1}`: trip slot `(b,k)` is used
- `g[w,b,k,s,p] in {0,1}`: trip `k` on bus `b` ends at shelter `s` and trip `k+1` starts at pickup `p`
- `start[w,b,k] >= 0`: boarding start time at the pickup
- `depart[w,b,k] >= 0`: departure time from the pickup after boarding
- `e[w,b,k,p,s] in Z_+`: evacuees carried on that trip
- `q[w,b,k,p,s] in Z_+`: wheelchair evacuees on that trip
- `r[w,b,k,p,s] in Z_+`: pets on that trip
- `unserved_e[w,p], unserved_q[w,p], unserved_r[w,p] >= 0`
- `sip_e[w,p], sip_q[w,p], sip_r[w,p] >= 0`

---
name: or-pyscipopt
description: Use when building, running, validating, or debugging Python optimization models that use PySCIPOpt and SCIP.
metadata:
  short-description: Build and run PySCIPOpt models
---

# OR PySCIPOpt

## Use when

- The task asks for a Python optimization model using SCIP or `pyscipopt`.
- Existing solver code imports `pyscipopt`, sets SCIP parameters, writes `.lp`/`.mps`, or calls a SCIP-based checker.
- The user asks to run, debug, speed up, validate, or explain a SCIP-based solve.

## Do not use when

- The task only needs a mathematical formulation and no solver-specific code.
- The project clearly uses a different solver stack, such as Pyomo with CBC/HiGHS, OR-Tools CP-SAT, PuLP, CPLEX, or cvxpy, unless the user asks to port it to PySCIPOpt.
- The model is nonlinear or stochastic in a way that SCIP cannot handle directly; first decide whether it can be linearized or approximated.

## Environment rule

Use the Python environment specified by the user or the project. In this ORClaw workspace, the default solver environment is the `or` conda environment with PySCIPOpt available:

```bash
conda run -n or python <script.py>
```

If `pyscipopt` is missing, report the missing dependency, identify the command that failed, and try the project-specified environment before changing solver stacks.

## Workflow

1. Read the task brief and input files before coding.
2. Write the full mathematical model first: sets, parameters, decision variables, objective, and constraints.
3. Implement the model with explicit tuple keys and named constraints.
4. Solve with the selected Python command.
5. Export the solution using the exact schema required by the task.
6. Validate with the task checker or an independent recomputation of feasibility and objective.
7. If the solution is not accepted, diagnose whether the issue is formulation, data parsing, export formatting, tolerance, or evaluator mismatch before changing the objective or constraints.

## PySCIPOpt patterns

- Use `from pyscipopt import Model, quicksum`.
- Create sparse variable dictionaries explicitly, for example:

```python
x = {(i, j, t): model.addVar(vtype="B", name=f"x[{i},{j},{t}]") for i, j, t in keys}
```

- Use `quicksum(...)` for large linear expressions.
- Use `model.addCons(expr <= rhs, name="capacity[k,t]")` for constraints.
- Use `model.setObjective(expr, "minimize")` or `model.setObjective(expr, "maximize")`.
- Set reproducibility and limits with SCIP parameters:

```python
model.setParam("limits/gap", 0.0005)
model.setParam("limits/time", time_limit)
model.setParam("display/verblevel", 0)
```

- After `model.optimize()`, inspect `model.getStatus()` and only read values if a solution exists.
- Read variable values with `model.getVal(x[key])`, not `x[key].X`.
- Read objective and bound with `model.getObjVal()` and `model.getDualbound()`.
- Read relative gap with `model.getGap()` when available.

## Modeling hygiene

- Calibrate every big-M from data. If a tight M is not obvious, derive a bound and document it in a short comment.
- Keep feasibility checks, objective recomputation, and reference-optimality comparison separate.
- Do not round exported decision values unless the schema explicitly requires it.
- For benchmark work, treat the evaluator or checker as the scoring authority and the reference solver as a validation baseline.
- Keep solver logs or a concise solve summary when the user needs auditability.
- Avoid changing data to make a model feasible unless the task is explicitly to design or repair an instance.

## Debugging boundaries

- If a model is infeasible, inspect hard lower bounds, conservation equations, and mutually exclusive binary logic before relaxing constraints.
- If objective values differ, recompute each objective component from exported decisions.
- If runtime is high, first remove dominated variables, tighten big-M values, add eligibility filters, and check whether binaries are really necessary.
- If SCIP returns an incumbent with a gap, say so clearly; do not call it exact optimality unless the status and gap support that claim.
- If an evaluator rejects a solution that the model considers feasible, replay the rejected rule directly from the exported file before changing solver code.

## Useful commands

```bash
python -c "import pyscipopt; print(pyscipopt.__version__)"
conda run -n or python -c "import pyscipopt; print(pyscipopt.__version__)"
<python-command> <solver-script.py>
<python-command> <checker-script.py> --solution <solution-file> --env-dir <task-dir>
```

import csv
import json
import math
import os
import sys
import time
from collections import defaultdict
from copy import deepcopy
from pathlib import Path

from pyscipopt import Model, quicksum, __version__ as PYSCIPOPT_VERSION


ROOT = Path("/app")
DATA_DIR = ROOT / "data"
SUBMISSIONS_DIR = ROOT / "submissions"
SOLUTION_PATH = SUBMISSIONS_DIR / "solution.json"
LOG_PATH = SUBMISSIONS_DIR / "solve_log.md"

KMAX = 4
MIP_GAP = 0.0005
TIME_BUFFER_SECONDS = 15.0


def read_csv(path):
    with open(path, newline="", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        return list(csv.DictReader(f))


def as_int(value):
    return int(float(value))


def as_float(value):
    return float(value)


def q8(value):
    rounded = round(float(value) + 0.0, 8)
    if abs(rounded) < 1e-9:
        rounded = 0.0
    return rounded


def load_data():
    with open(DATA_DIR / "config.json", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        config = json.load(f)

    depots = {}
    for row in read_csv(DATA_DIR / "depots.csv"):
        depots[row["depot_id"]] = {
            "depot_id": row["depot_id"],
            "name": row["name"],
            "stage_capacity": as_int(row["stage_capacity"]),
            "stage_cost_per_bus": as_float(row["stage_cost_per_bus"]),
            "release_delay_min": as_float(row["release_delay_min"]),
            "x": as_float(row["x"]),
            "y": as_float(row["y"]),
        }

    buses = {}
    for row in read_csv(DATA_DIR / "buses.csv"):
        buses[row["bus_id"]] = {
            "bus_id": row["bus_id"],
            "home_depot": row["home_depot"],
            "capacity": as_int(row["capacity"]),
            "wheelchair_slots": as_int(row["wheelchair_slots"]),
            "shift_limit_min": as_float(row["shift_limit_min"]),
            "operating_cost_per_min": as_float(row["operating_cost_per_min"]),
            "high_clearance": as_int(row["high_clearance"]),
            "medical_attendant": as_int(row["medical_attendant"]),
        }

    shelters = {}
    for row in read_csv(DATA_DIR / "shelters.csv"):
        shelters[row["shelter_id"]] = {
            "shelter_id": row["shelter_id"],
            "name": row["name"],
            "capacity": as_int(row["capacity"]),
            "wheelchair_capacity": as_int(row["wheelchair_capacity"]),
            "pet_capacity": as_int(row["pet_capacity"]),
            "medical_beds": as_float(row["medical_beds"]),
            "backup_power": as_int(row["backup_power"]),
            "smoke_safe_rating": as_int(row["smoke_safe_rating"]),
            "open_cost": as_float(row["open_cost"]),
            "x": as_float(row["x"]),
            "y": as_float(row["y"]),
        }

    pickups = {}
    for row in read_csv(DATA_DIR / "pickup_sites.csv"):
        pickups[row["pickup_id"]] = {
            "pickup_id": row["pickup_id"],
            "name": row["name"],
            "priority_class": row["priority_class"],
            "corridor_id": row["corridor_id"],
            "service_floor": as_float(row["service_floor"]),
            "boarding_bays": as_int(row["boarding_bays"]),
            "medical_share": as_float(row["medical_share"]),
            "shelter_in_place_limit_fraction": as_float(row["shelter_in_place_limit_fraction"]),
            "x": as_float(row["x"]),
            "y": as_float(row["y"]),
        }

    corridors = {}
    for row in read_csv(DATA_DIR / "corridors.csv"):
        corridors[row["corridor_id"]] = {
            "corridor_id": row["corridor_id"],
            "name": row["name"],
            "activation_cost": as_float(row["activation_cost"]),
            "travel_time_factor_if_active": as_float(row["travel_time_factor_if_active"]),
            "closure_extension_min": as_float(row["closure_extension_min"]),
            "capacity_bonus_trips": as_int(row["capacity_bonus_trips"]),
        }

    scenarios = {}
    for row in read_csv(DATA_DIR / "scenarios.csv"):
        scenarios[row["scenario_id"]] = {
            "scenario_id": row["scenario_id"],
            "probability": as_float(row["probability"]),
            "description": row["description"],
            "smoke_severity": as_float(row["smoke_severity"]),
            "global_travel_multiplier": as_float(row["global_travel_multiplier"]),
            "warning_time_min": as_float(row["warning_time_min"]),
            "min_shelter_smoke_rating": as_int(row["min_shelter_smoke_rating"]),
        }

    demand = defaultdict(dict)
    for row in read_csv(DATA_DIR / "scenario_pickup_demand.csv"):
        demand[row["scenario_id"]][row["pickup_id"]] = {
            "evacuees": as_int(row["evacuees"]),
            "wheelchair": as_int(row["wheelchair"]),
            "pets": as_int(row["pets"]),
        }

    corridor_impacts = defaultdict(dict)
    for row in read_csv(DATA_DIR / "scenario_corridor_impacts.csv"):
        corridor_impacts[row["scenario_id"]][row["corridor_id"]] = {
            "travel_multiplier": as_float(row["travel_multiplier"]),
            "capacity_trips": as_int(row["capacity_trips"]),
            "smoke_risk_multiplier": as_float(row["smoke_risk_multiplier"]),
            "latest_safe_departure_min": as_float(row["latest_safe_departure_min"]),
        }

    return {
        "config": config,
        "depots": depots,
        "buses": buses,
        "shelters": shelters,
        "pickups": pickups,
        "corridors": corridors,
        "scenarios": scenarios,
        "demand": demand,
        "corridor_impacts": corridor_impacts,
    }


def dist(a, b):
    return math.hypot(a["x"] - b["x"], a["y"] - b["y"])


def choose_first_stage(data):
    config = data["config"]
    shelters = data["shelters"]
    corridors = data["corridors"]
    depots = data["depots"]
    buses = data["buses"]
    pickups = data["pickups"]
    scenarios = data["scenarios"]
    demand = data["demand"]
    impacts = data["corridor_impacts"]

    open_shelters = sorted(
        shelter_id
        for shelter_id, shelter in shelters.items()
        if shelter["smoke_safe_rating"] >= 4
    )
    if len(open_shelters) != 4:
        raise RuntimeError("Expected exactly four smoke-safe shelters with rating >= 4.")

    active_corridors = sorted(corridors)
    if len(active_corridors) > config["max_active_corridors"]:
        raise RuntimeError("Instance-specific dominant contraflow choice exceeds active-corridor limit.")

    fixed_open_cost = sum(shelters[s]["open_cost"] for s in open_shelters)
    fixed_corridor_cost = sum(corridors[c]["activation_cost"] for c in active_corridors)

    model = Model("staging")
    model.setParam("display/verblevel", 0)
    model.setParam("limits/gap", MIP_GAP)

    z = {}
    for b in buses:
        for d in depots:
            z[b, d] = model.addVar(vtype="B", name=f"stage[{b},{d}]")

    proxy = {}
    for b, bus in buses.items():
        for d, depot in depots.items():
            weighted = 0.0
            for w, scenario in scenarios.items():
                total_dem = sum(demand[w][p]["evacuees"] for p in pickups)
                for p, pickup in pickups.items():
                    weight = scenario["probability"] * demand[w][p]["evacuees"] / total_dem
                    c = pickup["corridor_id"]
                    factor = (
                        scenario["global_travel_multiplier"]
                        * impacts[w][c]["travel_multiplier"]
                        * corridors[c]["travel_time_factor_if_active"]
                    )
                    base = (
                        config["base_intersection_delay_min"]
                        + config["minutes_per_distance_unit"] * dist(depot, pickup)
                    )
                    weighted += weight * factor * base
            proxy[b, d] = depots[d]["stage_cost_per_bus"] + bus["operating_cost_per_min"] * weighted

    for b in buses:
        model.addCons(quicksum(z[b, d] for d in depots) == 1, name=f"assign[{b}]")

    for d, depot in depots.items():
        model.addCons(
            quicksum(z[b, d] for b in buses) <= depot["stage_capacity"],
            name=f"depot_cap[{d}]",
        )

    model.addCons(
        quicksum(depots[d]["stage_cost_per_bus"] * z[b, d] for b in buses for d in depots)
        + fixed_open_cost
        + fixed_corridor_cost
        <= config["planning_budget"],
        name="planning_budget",
    )

    model.setObjective(quicksum(proxy[b, d] * z[b, d] for b in buses for d in depots), "minimize")
    model.optimize()

    if model.getNSols() == 0:
        raise RuntimeError("No feasible staging assignment found.")

    sol = model.getBestSol()
    staging = {}
    for b in buses:
        chosen = None
        for d in depots:
            if model.getSolVal(sol, z[b, d]) > 0.5:
                chosen = d
                break
        if chosen is None:
            raise RuntimeError(f"Staging missing for bus {b}.")
        staging[b] = chosen

    return {
        "open_shelters": open_shelters,
        "active_corridors": active_corridors,
        "staging": staging,
        "fixed_open_cost": fixed_open_cost,
        "fixed_corridor_cost": fixed_corridor_cost,
    }

---
name: advanced-mip-patterns
description: Use when a task involves fixed charges, logical implications, min/max choices, sequencing, no-overlap, piecewise costs, routing-state logic, or other advanced MIP modeling patterns.
metadata:
  short-description: Advanced MIP formulation patterns
---

# Advanced MIP Patterns

## Use when

- The formulation needs logical implications, fixed charges, activation decisions, ordering, no-overlap, disjunctions, min/max, absolute values, tiers, route state, or resource calendars.
- A simple LP/MIP is producing unrealistic behavior because decisions are not linked tightly enough.
- The model is slow or infeasible and the likely cause is weak big-M logic, excessive pairwise variables, or missing state transitions.

## Do not use when

- A continuous LP is sufficient and adding binaries would not represent a real decision.
- The task is better handled by a specialized CP-SAT, constraint programming, nonlinear, or simulation method and the user did not require MIP.
- The user only needs a high-level explanation; do not introduce advanced constructs unless they clarify the actual model.

## General boundary rules

- Add binaries only for real yes/no choices, disjunctions, or nonconvex structure.
- Prefer the tightest formulation that matches the business rule; avoid decorative binaries.
- Use solver-native constructs when they improve clarity and are supported by the target environment.
- If a construct makes the model much slower, compare it with a simpler valid alternative before keeping it.

## Fixed charge and activation

- Use a binary `y` for whether an activity/resource is active.
- Link activity with a tight bound: `x <= U * y`.
- Add lower activation only when the business requires minimum use: `x >= L * y`.
- Charge fixed cost once through `fixed_cost * y`, not per unit.
- Do not force `y = 1` whenever `x = 0` unless the activity is mandatory.
- If there are startup/shutdown costs over time, separate active state from transition state.

## Logical rules

- Prefer solver-native indicator constraints for simple if-then rules when they keep the model clearer.
- If using big-M, derive M from data bounds and keep one M per rule family when possible.
- For either-or choices, use binaries whose sum is exactly one or at most one, matching the business rule.
- Distinguish implication (`A -> B`) from equivalence (`A <-> B`); the reverse direction often needs a separate constraint.
- For conditional capacity, link both activity and resource use to the condition.

## Min, max, absolute value

- For `z = max(a, b)`, add `z >= a`, `z >= b`, and objective pressure if minimizing z. Use binaries only when equality is needed without objective pressure.
- For absolute deviation `d = |x - target|`, add `d >= x - target` and `d >= target - x`.
- For service-level penalties, separate hard feasibility from soft penalty variables.
- For `min`, either transform with signs or add binaries when equality is not enforced by the objective.
- For ratio constraints, cross-multiply only when denominators are constant or safely bounded away from zero.

## Sequencing and no-overlap

- For jobs sharing a resource, use order binary `before[i,j]`.
- Typical pattern:
  - `start[j] >= end[i] - M * (1 - before[i,j])`
  - `start[i] >= end[j] - M * before[i,j]`
- Compute M from the scheduling horizon, not from an arbitrary large number.
- Include setup, cleanup, travel, rest, or turnover time in the correct side of the ordering constraint.
- Create pairwise order variables only for pairs that can share a resource or conflict.
- If time is discrete and the horizon is small, a time-indexed model may be cleaner than pairwise disjunctions.

## Piecewise and tiers

- Use solver-native piecewise-linear features for convex piecewise costs when suitable.
- For nonconvex tiers, use explicit segment variables and segment activation binaries.
- Ensure tier quantities sum to the original activity and each tier has its own upper bound.
- Distinguish incremental tiers from all-units tiers; their formulations and costs differ.
- Check whether discount tiers create nonconvex incentives that require binary selection.

## State and inventory logic

- State variables need initial conditions, transition equations, and terminal requirements if the business specifies them.
- Route or schedule state should be advanced in event order, not checked only row by row.
- Recovery, restock, cleaning, cooldown, maintenance, and rest rules often require cumulative state or last-event logic.
- If state can reset, model the reset trigger and its resource/time cost.

## Debugging advanced models

- First solve a relaxed or smaller instance and inspect whether the intended binaries activate.
- Add named constraints so IIS output points to business rules.
- If the reference solution is infeasible, replay the relevant state transitions manually before changing the evaluator.
- If the LP relaxation is very weak, inspect big-M values, missing lower/upper links, and symmetry.
- If the model is unexpectedly infeasible, test each advanced rule family on a tiny instance with a known feasible solution.

/bin/bash -lc "PYTHONPATH=/opt/or-deps python - <<'PY'
import itertools, json, math, time
from submissions.solve import load_data, audit_solution, solve_scenario_greedy, build_solution

data = load_data()
with open('/app/submissions/solution.json') as f:
    incumbent = json.load(f)
first_stage = {
    'open_shelters': [r['shelter_id'] for r in incumbent['shelters'] if r['open']],
    'active_corridors': [r['corridor_id'] for r in incumbent['contraflow'] if r['active']],
    'staging': {r['bus_id']: r['depot_id'] for r in incumbent['staging']},
}

def eval_fs(fs):
    scenario_results = {}
    for sid in sorted(data['scenarios']):
        scenario_results[sid] = solve_scenario_greedy(data, sid, fs)
    sol = build_solution(data, fs, scenario_results)
    audit = audit_solution(data, sol)
    return audit, sol

start = time.time()
audit, _ = eval_fs(first_stage)
print('baseline', audit['total_cost'], len(audit['errors']), 'time', time.time()-start)

buses = sorted(data['buses'])
depots = sorted(data['depots'])
cap = {d: data['depots'][d]['stage_capacity'] for d in depots}
base_counts = {d: sum(1 for b in buses if first_stage['staging'][b]==d) for d in depots}

best = (audit['total_cost'], None)
count = 0
for b in buses:
    cur = first_stage['staging'][b]
    for d in depots:
        if d == cur: continue
        counts = dict(base_counts)
        if counts[d] + 1 > cap[d]:
            continue
        counts[cur] -= 1; counts[d] += 1
        fs = dict(first_stage)
        fs['staging'] = dict(first_stage['staging'])
        fs['staging'][b] = d
        count += 1
        audit2, _ = eval_fs(fs)
        if not audit2['errors'] and audit2['total_cost'] < best[0] - 1e-8:
            best = (audit2['total_cost'], ('move', b, cur, d))
            print('improve1', best)
print('searched', count, 'best', best, 'elapsed', time.time()-start)

/bin/bash -lc "PYTHONPATH=/opt/or-deps python - <<'PY'
import json, time
from submissions.solve import load_data, audit_solution, solve_scenario_greedy, build_solution

data = load_data()
with open('/app/submissions/solution.json') as f:
    incumbent = json.load(f)
fs = {
    'open_shelters': [r['shelter_id'] for r in incumbent['shelters'] if r['open']],
    'active_corridors': [r['corridor_id'] for r in incumbent['contraflow'] if r['active']],
    'staging': {r['bus_id']: r['depot_id'] for r in incumbent['staging']},
}

def eval_fs(fs):
    scenario_results = {}
    for sid in sorted(data['scenarios']):
        scenario_results[sid] = solve_scenario_greedy(data, sid, fs)
    sol = build_solution(data, fs, scenario_results)
    audit = audit_solution(data, sol)
    return audit, sol

buses = sorted(data['buses'])
depots = sorted(data['depots'])
cap = {d: data['depots'][d]['stage_capacity'] for d in depots}

cur_audit, cur_sol = eval_fs(fs)
print('start', cur_audit['total_cost'], fs['staging'])
step = 0
while True:
    step += 1
    counts = {d: sum(1 for b in buses if fs['staging'][b]==d) for d in depots}
    best_audit = cur_audit
    best_sol = cur_sol
    best_move = None
    best_fs = fs
    for b in buses:
        cur = fs['staging'][b]
        for d in depots:
            if d == cur:
                continue
            if counts[d] + 1 > cap[d]:
                continue
            cand_fs = {
                'open_shelters': fs['open_shelters'],
                'active_corridors': fs['active_corridors'],
                'staging': dict(fs['staging'])
            }
            cand_fs['staging'][b] = d
            audit, sol = eval_fs(cand_fs)
            if audit['errors']:
                continue
            if audit['total_cost'] < best_audit['total_cost'] - 1e-8:
                best_audit = audit
                best_sol = sol
                best_move = (b, cur, d)
                best_fs = cand_fs
    if best_move is None:
        print('local optimum', cur_audit['total_cost'], fs['staging'])
        break
    fs = best_fs
    cur_audit = best_audit
    cur_sol = best_sol
    print('step', step, 'move', best_move, 'obj', cur_audit['total_cost'], fs['staging'])

import itertools, json, time
from submissions.solve import load_data, audit_solution, solve_scenario_greedy, build_solution

data = load_data()
fs = {
    'open_shelters': ['S1','S2','S3','S5'],
    'active_corridors': ['C1','C2','C3','C4','C5'],
    'staging': {'B01': 'D3', 'B02': 'D3', 'B03': 'D2', 'B04': 'D1', 'B05': 'D2', 'B06': 'D3', 'B07': 'D3', 'B08': 'D2', 'B09': 'D2', 'B10': 'D1'}
}

def eval_fs(fs):
    scenario_results = {}
    for sid in sorted(data['scenarios']):
        scenario_results[sid] = solve_scenario_greedy(data, sid, fs)
    sol = build_solution(data, fs, scenario_results)
    audit = audit_solution(data, sol)
    return audit, sol

cur_audit, _ = eval_fs(fs)
print('baseline', cur_audit['total_cost'])
buses = sorted(data['buses'])
depots = sorted(data['depots'])
cap = {d: data['depots'][d]['stage_capacity'] for d in depots}
base_counts = {d: sum(1 for b in buses if fs['staging'][b]==d) for d in depots}
count=0; best=(cur_audit['total_cost'], None)
start=time.time()
for b1,b2 in itertools.combinations(buses,2):
    cur1=fs['staging'][b1]; cur2=fs['staging'][b2]
    for d1 in depots:
        for d2 in depots:
            if d1==cur1 and d2==cur2:
                continue
            counts=dict(base_counts)
            counts[cur1]-=1; counts[cur2]-=1; counts[d1]+=1; counts[d2]+=1
            if any(counts[d] > cap[d] for d in depots):
                continue
            cand_fs={'open_shelters':fs['open_shelters'],'active_corridors':fs['active_corridors'],'staging':dict(fs['staging'])}
            cand_fs['staging'][b1]=d1; cand_fs['staging'][b2]=d2
            count+=1
            audit,_=eval_fs(cand_fs)
            if audit['errors']:
                continue
            if audit['total_cost'] < best[0] - 1e-8:
                best=(audit['total_cost'], (b1,cur1,d1,b2,cur2,d2))
                print('improve', best, 'elapsed', time.time()-start)
print('count', count, 'best', best, 'elapsed', time.time()-start)

import time
from submissions.solve import load_data, audit_solution, solve_scenario_greedy, build_solution

data = load_data()
fs = {
    'open_shelters': ['S1','S2','S3','S5'],
    'active_corridors': ['C1','C2','C3','C4','C5'],
    'staging': {'B01':'D3','B02':'D3','B03':'D2','B04':'D2','B05':'D2','B06':'D3','B07':'D3','B08':'D2','B09':'D1','B10':'D1'}
}

def eval_fs(fs):
    scenario_results = {}
    for sid in sorted(data['scenarios']):
        scenario_results[sid] = solve_scenario_greedy(data, sid, fs)
    sol = build_solution(data, fs, scenario_results)
    audit = audit_solution(data, sol)
    return audit, sol

buses = sorted(data['buses'])
depots = sorted(data['depots'])
cap = {d:data['depots'][d]['stage_capacity'] for d in depots}
cur_audit, cur_sol = eval_fs(fs)
print('start', cur_audit['total_cost'])
step=0

    counts={d:sum(1 for b in buses if fs['staging'][b]==d) for d in depots}
    best_audit=cur_audit; best_fs=fs; best_move=None
    for b in buses:
        cur=fs['staging'][b]
        for d in depots:
            if d==cur or counts[d]+1>cap[d]:

            cand_fs={'open_shelters':fs['open_shelters'],'active_corridors':fs['active_corridors'],'staging':dict(fs['staging'])}

    if best_move is None:
        print('local optimum', cur_audit['total_cost'], fs['staging'])
        break
    step += 1
    fs=best_fs; cur_audit=best_audit
    print('step', step, best_move, cur_audit['total_cost'], fs['staging'])
